Title: Journal asiatique, ou recueil de mémoires, d'extraits et de notices relatifs à l'histoire, à la philosophie, aux langues et à la littérature des peuples orientaux, huitième série, tome XIV (juillet-décembre 1889); it carries Jules Perruchon, Histoire des guerres d'Amda Syon, roi d'Éthiopie, traduite de l'éthiopien
Creator: Société asiatique (periodical volume); the chronicle of the wars of King Amda Syon (reigned 1314 to 1344 by the dating Perruchon adopts) in French translation and Ge'ez text by Jules Perruchon, pp. 271 to 493
Publisher: Paris: Imprimerie nationale, 1889 (Journal asiatique, huitième série, tome XIV)
Publication Date: 1889
Rights: Public Domain
Rights basis: title page read in the OCR gives 'Huitième série, tome XIV ... M DCCC LXXXIX'; the Internet Archive item record carries no rights field
Language: fra (mixed: French periodical volume; the Amda Syon article gives the French translation and the Ge'ez text)
Source: Internet Archive item in.ernet.dli.2015.530741 (archive.org/details/in.ernet.dli.2015.530741), full-text file 2015.530741.journal-asiatique_djvu.txt, fetched directly by the Library thread, October 2026.
Provenance note: The OCR text follows unchanged apart from this header (Digital Library of India scan, Central Archaeological Library, New Delhi). Nothing was stripped, normalised, corrected, re-spaced or retyped. OCR sampled at opening, middle and end: the French reads with scattered errors (for example 'Amda Sion', 'Syon' and 'Sy6n' for the same name, broken apostrophes); the Ge'ez lines are garbled; the volume's other articles are present and untouched.
Content note: A mixed periodical volume. The relevant article is Perruchon's 'Histoire des guerres d'Amda Syon' in the September-October issue, running from file line 12095 (journal p. 271) to about line 22913 (p. 493). It gives a French translation of the Ethiopian royal chronicle of the wars of Amda Syon against the Muslim sultanates and the rebellious provinces, with the Ge'ez text and notes. It is a court record of the first Solomonic conqueror, in the reign just before the window's monastic expansion took its mature form. The rest of the volume is other orientalist articles and is out of scope. Second-grade OCR: verify every quotation against the page image.

La flute droile, etant courte, devait avoir des sons

nous n’avons rieii Irouve, dans les ouvrages chinois, qui ait pu 
nous en donner une id6e exacte. Nous supposons qu’ils ^talent 
Scbiites ou peut-^tre Sofs, autrement mystiques. Cette dernikre 
bypothese nous est venue a la suite d’un entretien que nous avons 
eu, en 1876, avec un Salar de H 6 -tcheoUj etabli a Canton depuis 
un certain temps , et qui , menant une vie asc^tbjue , professait une 
sorte de pantheism e et repoussait le dogme musulman. Malheureuse- 
ment,, comma cet bom me etait peu intelligent, nous n’osons rien 
jconclure. (P. Dabry de Tbiersant, Le mahomSlisme en Chine et dans 
le Turkestan oriental j 1878, t. I, p. a 34 ).
